# Reuse taxonomy mapping — v9 connection expansion batch 06 completion sweep (Markdown only)

Generated: 2026-06-02

Scope: continue expanding URL-backed and review-tracked project → component → taxonomy connections. This batch is intentionally Markdown-only and does not prepare import files.

## Batch summary

- Edge rows: **617**
- Projects touched: **26**
- Component-group candidates touched: **94**
- Project-level method rows: **53**
- Evidence-backed project rows: **13 projects**
- Review-only / direct-source-gap project rows: **13 projects**
- `import_ready` is kept `FALSE` for every row because the current phase is evidence expansion, not import preparation.
- No optional/PropertyOption-style nodes are introduced; all mapping targets stay within the cleaned six-node-per-type taxonomy.
- `REVIEW_ONLY` rows are deliberately included to keep every remaining topology project visible, but they must not be imported until direct evidence URLs are added.

## Taxonomy target set used

- Wiederverwendungsergebnis: `Bestandserhalt`, `Wiederverwendung_gleiche_Funktion`, `Wiederverwendung_neue_Funktion`, `Modul_oder_Abschnittswiederverwendung`, `Material_Reprocessing`, `Geplant_oder_Gelagert`
- Quelle: `Eigener_Bestand`, `Externer_Spenderbau`, `Lager_und_Bauteilboerse`, `Baustellenrest_oder_Ueberproduktion`, `Restposten_Abfall_Unbekannt`, `Nicht_bestimmbar`
- Wiederverwendungsort: `In_situ`, `Im_selben_Gebaeude_versetzt`, `Auf_demselben_Areal`, `Lokal_oder_Regional_importiert`, `Extern_importiert`, `Gelagert_oder_Unbekannt`
- Aufbereitungsverfahren: `Reinigung_und_Oberflaeche`, `Reparatur_und_Refurbishment`, `Zuschnitt_und_Anpassung`, `Pruefung_Sortierung_QS`, `Rekonfiguration_und_Vormontage`, `Keine_wesentliche_Aufbereitung`
- Rueckbauverfahren: `Ausbau_von_Bauteilen`, `Demontage_von_Modulen`, `Schneidender_Rueckbau`, `Sortierung_und_Bergung`, `Dekonstruktion_mit_Inventar`, `Nicht_dokumentiert`
- Methode: `Bestands_und_ReUse_Assessment`, `Urban_Mining_und_Scouting`, `Verfuegbarkeitsbasiertes_Design`, `Design_for_Disassembly`, `Dokumentation_und_Monitoring`, `Zirkulaere_Beschaffung`

## Review queue from this batch

These projects are now structurally mapped to the cleaned taxonomy but still need direct source capture before any import action:

- `p_berlin_schildow_pilot_house` — Berlin-Schildow Pilot House / Berlin-Schildow 2nd pilot house: Direct source URL needed before import; do not treat as final evidence.
- `p_bestandverplanzung_pavilion_muenchen` — Bestandverplanzung Pavilion, München: Direct source URL needed before import; do not treat as final evidence.
- `p_broethen_twin_house_hoyerswerda` — Broethen Twin-House, Hoyerswerda: Direct source URL needed before import; do not treat as final evidence.
- `p_haus_hos_mehrfamilienhaus_muehlhausen` — Haus HOS / Mehrfamilienhaus Mühlhausen: Direct source URL needed before import; do not treat as final evidence.

- `p_mehrow_pilot_house` — Mehrow Pilot House: Direct source URL needed before import; do not treat as final evidence.
- `p_musee_de_folklore_mouscron` — Musée de Folklore Vie Frontalière / MUSEF Mouscron: Direct source URL needed before import.
- `p_plattenpalast_berlin` — Plattenpalast Berlin: Direct source URL needed before import.
- `p_plattenvereinigung_berlin` — Plattenvereinigung Berlin: Direct source URL needed before import.
- `p_resilience_la_ferme_des_possibles_stains` — Résilience / La Ferme des Possibles Stains: Direct source URL needed before import.
- `p_verbiest_karreveld_brussels` — Verbiest + Karreveld: Direct source URL needed before import.
- `p_association_house_groeditz` — Association house, Gröditz: Direct source URL needed before import.
- `p_association_house_plauen` — Association house, Plauen: Direct source URL needed before import.

## Suggested next pass

1. Resolve the direct-source gaps for the older German concrete-panel reuse projects as one group: Schildow, Broethen, Haus HOS, Mehrow, Gröditz and Plauen.
2. Resolve the Brussels/French/Belgian cultural-project gaps as one group: MUSEF Mouscron, Résilience, Verbiest/Karreveld, Plattenpalast and Plattenvereinigung.
3. After URLs are captured, convert only rows with direct source support from `REVIEW_ONLY` to `ADD`; keep all rows `import_ready = FALSE` until the final import-prep step.
